Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for Dual Connectivity Procedures > SENB RELEASE CONFIRM
Content: This message is sent by the MeNB to confirm the release of all resources for a specific UE at the SeNB. Direction: MeNB $ arrow $ SeNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for Dual Connectivity Procedures > SENB COUNTER CHECK REQUEST
Content: This message is sent by the SeNB to request the verification of the value of the PDCP COUNTs associated with SCG bearers established in the SeNB. Direction: SeNB $ arrow $ MeNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > SGNB ADDITION REQUEST
Content: This message is sent by the MeNB to the en-gNB to request the preparation of resources for EN-DC operation for a specific UE Direction: MeNB $ arrow $ en-gNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > SGNB ADDITION REQUEST ACKNOWLEDGE
Content: This message is sent by the en-gNB to confirm the MeNB about the SgNB addition preparation. Direction: en-gNB $ arrow $ MeNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > SGNB ADDITION REQUEST REJECT
Content: This message is sent by the en-gNB to inform the MeNB that the SgNB Addition Preparation has failed. Direction: en-gNB $ arrow $ MeNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > SGNB RECONFIGURATION COMPLETE
Content: This message is sent by the MeNB to the en-gNB to indicate whether the configuration requested by the en-gNB was applied by the UE. Direction: MeNB $ arrow $ en-gNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > SGNB MODIFICATION REQUEST
Content: This message is sent by the MeNB to the en-gNB to request the preparation to modify en-gNB resources for a specific UE, to query for the current SCG configuration, or to provide the S-RLF-related information to the en-gNB. Direction: MeNB $ arrow $ en-gNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > SGNB MODIFICATION REQUEST ACKNOWLEDGE
Content: This message is sent by the en-gNB to confirm the MeNB’s request to modify the en-gNB resources for a specific UE. Direction: en-gNB $ arrow $ MeNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > SGNB MODIFICATION REQUEST REJECT
Content: This message is sent by the en-gNB to inform the MeNB that the MeNB initiated SgNB Modification Preparation has failed. Direction: en-gNB $ arrow $ MeNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > SGNB MODIFICATION REQUIRED
Content: This message is sent by the en-gNB to the MeNB to request the modification of en-gNB resources for a specific UE. Direction: en-gNB $ arrow $ MeNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > SGNB MODIFICATION CONFIRM
Content: This message is sent by the MeNB to inform the en-gNB about the successful modification. Direction: MeNB $ arrow $ en-gNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > SGNB MODIFICATION REFUSE
Content: This message is sent by the MeNB to inform the en-gNB that the SgNB initiated SgNB Modification has failed. Direction: MeNB $ arrow $ en-gNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > SGNB RELEASE REQUEST
Content: This message is sent by the MeNB to the en-gNB to request the release of resources. Direction: MeNB $ arrow $ en-gNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > SGNB RELEASE REQUEST ACKNOWLEDGE
Content: This message is sent by the en-gNB to the MeNB to confirme the request to release en-gNB resources. Direction: en-gNB $ arrow $ MeNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > SGNB RELEASE REQUEST REJECT
Content: This message is sent by the en-gNB to the MeNB to reject the request to release en-gNB resources. Direction: en-gNB $ arrow $ MeNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > SGNB RELEASE REQUIRED
Content: This message is sent by the en-gNB to request the release of all resources for a specific UE at the en-gNB. Direction: en-gNB $ arrow $ MeNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > SGNB RELEASE CONFIRM
Content: This message is sent by the MeNB to confirm the release of all resources for a specific UE at the en-gNB. Direction: MeNB $ arrow $ en-gNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > SGNB COUNTER CHECK REQUEST
Content: This message is sent by the en-gNB to request the verification of the value of the PDCP COUNTs associated with the bearers established in the en-gNB. Direction: en-gNB $ arrow $ MeNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > SGNB CHANGE REQUIRED
Content: This message is sent by the en-gNB to the MeNB to request the change of en-gNB for a specific UE. Direction: en-gNB $ arrow $ MeNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > SGNB CHANGE CONFIRM
Content: This message is sent by the MeNB to inform the en-gNB about the successful change. Direction: MeNB $ arrow $ en-gNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > SGNB CHANGE REFUSE
Content: This message is sent by the MeNB to inform the en-gNB that the SgNB initiated SgNB Change has failed. Direction: MeNB $ arrow $ en-gNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > SECONDARY RAT DATA USAGE REPORT
Content: This message is sent by the en-gNB to report data volumes for secondary RAT. Direction: en-gNB $ arrow $ MeNB
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > RRC TRANSFER
Content: This message is sent by the MeNB to the en-gNB or by the en-gNB to the MeNB to transfer an RRC message. Direction: MeNB $ arrow $ en-gNB or en-gNB $ arrow $ MeNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > PARTIAL RESET REQUIRED
Content: This message is sent by an initiating node to a neighbouring node, both nodes able to interact for EN-DC, to release all the resources for selected UEs. Direction: en-gNB $ arrow $ MeNB, MeNB $ arrow $ en-gNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > PARTIAL RESET CONFIRM
Content: This message is sent by an initiating node to a neighbouring node, both nodes able to interact for EN-DC, to confirm the release all the resources for selected UEs. Direction: en-gNB $ arrow $ MeNB, MeNB $ arrow $ en-gNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > E-UTRA NR CELL RESOURCE COORDINATION REQUEST
Content: Direction: eNB $ arrow $ en-gNB, en-gNB $ arrow $ eNB. This message is sent by a neighbouring eNB to a peer en-gNB or by a neighbouring en-gNB to a peer eNB, both nodes able to interact for EN-DC, to express the desired resource allocation for data traffic, for the sake of E-UTRA - NR Cell Resource Coordination.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > E-UTRA NR CELL RESOURCE COORDINATION RESPONSE
Content: This message is sent by a neighbouring eNB to a peer en-gNB or by a neighbouring en-gNB to a peer eNB, both nodes able to interact for EN-DC, as a response to the E-UTRA NR CELL RESOURCE COORDINATION REQUEST. Direction: eNB $ arrow $ en-gNB, en-gNB $ arrow $ eNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > SGNB ACTIVITY NOTIFICATION
Content: This message is sent by the en-gNB to inform the MeNB that resources for E-RABs controlled by the en-gNB have not been used or are in use again. Direction: en-gNB $ arrow $ MeNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > GNB STATUS INDICATION
Content: This message is sent by the en-gNB to indicate to the eNB its status of overload. Direction: en-gNB $ arrow $ eNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > TRACE START
Content: This message is sent by the MeNB to initiate a trace session for a UE. Direction: MeNB $ arrow $ en-gNB
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > DEACTIVATE TRACE
Content: This message is sent by the MeNB to deactivate a trace session. Direction: MeNB $ arrow $ en-gNB
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > UE Radio Capability ID Mapping Request
Content: This message is sent by the en-gNB and is used to request the UE Radio Capability information that maps to a specific UE Radio Capability ID. Direction: en-gNB $ arrow $ eNB
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > UE Radio Capability ID Mapping Response
Content: This message is sent by the eNB and is used to provide the UE Radio Capability information that maps to a specific UE Radio Capability ID indicated in the UE RADIO CAPABILITY ID MAPPING REQUEST message. Direction: eNB $ arrow $ en-gNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for E-UTRAN-NR Dual Connectivity Procedures > CONDITIONAL PSCELL CHANGE CANCEL
Content: This message is sent by the MeNB to the source en-gNB to inform the cancellation of all of the prepared PSCells in the target en-gNB during a Conditional PSCell Change. Direction: MeNB $ arrow $ en-gNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message Functional Definition and Content > Messages for IAB Procedures > F1-C TRAFFIC TRANSFER
Content: This message is sent by the en-gNB to the MeNB or by the MeNB to the en-gNB to transfer the F1-C traffic to and from an IAB-node. Direction: MeNB $ arrow $ en-gNB or en-gNB $ arrow $ MeNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > General
Content: When specifying information elements which are to be represented by bit strings, if not otherwise specifically stated in the semantics description of the concerned IE or elsewhere, the following principle applies with regards to the ordering of bits: The first bit (leftmost bit) contains the most significant bit (MSB); The last bit (rightmost bit) contains the least significant bit (LSB); When importing bit strings from other specifications, the first bit of the bit string contains the first bit of the concerned information.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > GTP Tunnel Endpoint
Content: The GTP Tunnel Endpoint IE identifies an X2 transport bearer or the S-GW endpoint of the S1 transport bearer associated to an E-RAB. It contains a Transport Layer Address and a GTP Tunnel Endpoint Identifier. The Transport Layer Address is an IP address to be used for the X2 user plane transport (see TS 36.424 ) or for the S1 user plane transport (see TS 36.414 ). The GTP Tunnel Endpoint Identifier is to be used for the user plane transport. The QoS Mapping Information is used to set the IP header of packets in case that the en-gNB serves the IAB, and the packets belonging to MN-terminated split bearer/SCG bearer are transmitted from MeNB to en-gNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Trace Activation
Content: Defines parameters related to trace activation.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Handover Restriction List
Content: This IE defines roaming or access restrictions for subsequent mobility action for which the eNB provides information about the target of the mobility action towards the UE, e.g., handover and CCO, or for SCG selection during dual connectivity operation.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > PLMN Identity
Content: This information element indicates the PLMN Identity.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > DL Forwarding
Content: This element indicates that the E-RAB is proposed for forwarding of downlink packets.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Cause
Content: The purpose of the cause information element is to indicate the reason for a particular event for the whole protocol. The meaning of the different cause values is described in the following table. In general, "not supported" cause values indicate that the concerned capability is missing. On the other hand, "not available" cause values indicate that the concerned capability is present, but insufficient resources were available to perform the requested action.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Criticality Diagnostics
Content: The Criticality Diagnostics IE is sent by the eNB/en-gNB when parts of a received message have not been comprehended or were missing, or if the message contained logical errors. When applicable, it contains information about which IEs were not comprehended or were missing.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Served Cell Information
Content: This IE contains cell configuration information of a cell that a neighbour eNB may need for the X2 AP interface.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > E-RAB Level QoS Parameters
Content: This IE defines the QoS to be applied to an E-RAB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > GBR QoS Information
Content: This IE indicates the maximum and guaranteed bit rates of a GBR E-RAB for downlink and uplink. NOTE: For LTE DC, the SeNB regards the GBR QoS Information IE as an E-RAB level parameter also for E-RABs configured with the split bearer option, although for the split bearer option the bitrates signalled by the MeNB are typically not equal to the bitrates signalled by the MME for that E-RAB (see TS 36.300 ).
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Bit Rate
Content: This IE indicates the number of bits delivered by E-UTRAN in UL or to E-UTRAN in DL or by UE in sidelink within a period of time, divided by the duration of the period. It is used, for example, to indicate the maximum or guaranteed bit rate for a GBR E-RAB, or an aggregated maximum bit rate.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > UE Aggregate Maximum Bit Rate
Content: On Handover Aggregate Maximum Bitrate is transferred to the target eNB. In Dual Connectivity, UE Aggregate Maximum Bit Rate is split into MeNB UE Aggregate Maximum Bit Rate and SeNB UE Aggregate Maximum Bit Rate which are enforced by MeNB and SeNB respectively as specified in TS 36.300 . The UE Aggregate Maximum Bitrate is applicable for all Non-GBR bearers per UE which is defined for the Downlink and the Uplink direction and provided by the MME to the eNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Message Type
Content: The Message Type IE uniquely identifies the message being sent. It is mandatory for all messages.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > ECGI
Content: The E-UTRAN Cell Global Identifier (ECGI) is used to globally identify a cell (see TS 36.401 ).
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > COUNT Value
Content: This information element indicates the 12 bit PDCP sequence number and the corresponding 20 bit Hyper frame number.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > GUMMEI
Content: This information element indicates the globally unique MME identity.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > UL Interference Overload Indication
Content: This IE provides, per PRB, a report on interference overload. The interaction between the indication of UL Interference Overload and UL High Interference is implementation specific.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > UL High Interference Indication
Content: This IE provides, per PRB, a 2 level report on interference sensitivity. The interaction between the indication of UL Overload and UL High Interference is implementation specific.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Relative Narrowband Tx Power (RNTP)
Content: This IE provides an indication on DL power restriction per PRB or per subframe per PRB (Enhanced RNTP) in a cell and other information needed by a neighbour eNB for interference aware scheduling.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > GU Group Id
Content: The GU Group Id IE is the globally unique group id corresponding to a pool area.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Location Reporting Information
Content: This information element indicates how the location information should be reported.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Global eNB ID
Content: This IE is used to globally identify an eNB (see TS 36.401 ).
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > E-RAB ID
Content: This IE uniquely identifies an E-RAB for a UE.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > eNB UE X2AP ID
Content: This information element, combined with the eNB UE X2AP ID Extension when present regardless its value, uniquely identifies an UE over the X2 interface within an eNB. The usage of this IE is defined in TS 36.401 . NOTE: If X2-C signalling transport is shared among multiple interface instances, the value of the eNB UE X2AP ID, combined with the eNB UE X2AP ID Extension, if applicable, is allocated so that it can be associated with an X2-C interface instance.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Subscriber Profile ID for RAT/Frequency priority
Content: The Subscriber Profile ID IE for RAT/Frequency Selection Priority is used to define camp priorities in Idle mode and to control inter-RAT/inter-frequency handover in Active mode (TS 36.300 ). 9.2.25a Additional RRM Policy Index The Additional RRM Policy Index IE is used to provide additional information independent from the Subscriber Profile ID for RAT/Frequency priority as specified in TS 36.300 .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > EARFCN
Content: The E-UTRA Absolute Radio Frequency Channel Number defines the carrier frequency used in a cell for a given direction (UL or DL) in FDD or for both UL and DL directions in TDD.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Transmission Bandwidth
Content: The Transmission Bandwidth IE is used to indicate the UL or DL transmission bandwidth expressed in units of resource blocks " N " (TS 36.104 ). The values bw1, bw6, bw15, bw25, bw50, bw75, bw100 correspond to the number of resource blocks "N " 6, 15, 25, 50, 75, 100.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > E-RAB List
Content: The IE contains a list of E-RAB identities with a cause value. It is used for example to indicate not admitted bearers.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > UE Security Capabilities
Content: The UE Security Capabilities IE defines the supported algorithms for encryption and integrity protection in the UE. The eNBs store and send the complete bitmaps without modification or truncation as specified in TS 36.300 .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > AS Security Information
Content: The AS Security Information IE is used to generate the key material to be used for AS security with the UE.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Allocation and Retention Priority
Content: This IE specifies the relative importance compared to other E-RABs for allocation and retention of the E-UTRAN Radio Access Bearer.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Time To Wait
Content: This IE defines the minimum allowed waiting times.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > SRVCC Operation Possible
Content: The IE indicates that both the UE and the MME are SRVCC-capable. E-UTRAN behaviour on reception of this is specified in TS 23.216 .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Hardware Load Indicator
Content: The Hardware Load Indicator IE indicates the status of the Hardware Load experienced by the cell.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > S1 TNL Load Indicator
Content: The S1 TNL Load Indicator IE indicates the status of the S1 Transport Network Load experienced by the cell.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Load Indicator
Content: The Load Indicator IE indicates the status of Load.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Radio Resource Status
Content: The Radio Resource Status IE indicates the usage of the PRBs for all traffic in Downlink and Uplink (TS 36.314 , TS 23.203 ) and the usage of PDCCH CCEs for Downlink and Uplink scheduling.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > UE History Information
Content: The UE History Information IE contains information about cells that a UE has been served by in active state prior to the target cell. The overall mechanism is described in TS 36.300 . NOTE: The definition of this IE is aligned with the definition of the UE History Information IE in TS 36.413 .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Last Visited Cell Information
Content: The Last Visited Cell Information may contain cell specific information.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Last Visited E-UTRAN Cell Information
Content: The Last Visited E-UTRAN Cell Information contains information about a cell that is to be used for RRM purposes.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Last Visited GERAN Cell Information
Content: The Last Visited Cell Information for GERAN is currently undefined. NOTE: If in later Releases this is defined, the choice type may be extended with the actual GERAN specific information.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Cell Type
Content: The cell type provides the cell coverage area.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Number of Antenna Ports
Content: The Number of Antenna Ports IE is used to indicate the number of cell specific antenna ports.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Composite Available Capacity Group
Content: The Composite Available Capacity Group IE indicates the overall available resource level in the cell in Downlink and Uplink.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Composite Available Capacity
Content: The Composite Available Capacity IE indicates the overall available resource level in the cell in either Downlink or Uplink.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Cell Capacity Class Value
Content: The Cell Capacity Class Value IE indicates the value that classifies the cell capacity with regards to the other cells. The Cell Capacity Class Value IE only indicates resources that are configured for traffic purposes.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Capacity Value
Content: The Capacity Value IE indicates the amount of resources that are available relative to the total E-UTRAN resources. The capacity value should be measured and reported so that the minimum E-UTRAN resource usage of existing services is reserved according to implementation. The Capacity Value IE can be weighted according to the ratio of cell capacity class values, if available.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Mobility Parameters Information
Content: The Mobility Parameters Information IE contains the change of the Handover Trigger as compared to its current value. The Handover Trigger corresponds to the threshold at which a cell initialises the handover preparation procedure towards a specific neighbour cell. Positive value of the change means the handover is proposed to take place later.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Mobility Parameters Modification Range
Content: The Mobility Parameters Modification Range IE contains the range of Handover Trigger Change values permitted by the eNB at the moment the MOBILITY CHANGE FAILURE message is sent.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > PRACH Configuration
Content: This IE indicates the PRACH resources used in neighbor cell.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Subframe Allocation
Content: The Subframe Allocation IE is used to indicate the subframes that are allocated for MBSFN within the radio frame allocation period as defined in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > CSG Membership Status
Content: This element indicates the membership status of the UE to a particular CSG.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > CSG ID
Content: This element indicates the identifier of the Closed Subscriber Group.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > ABS Information
Content: This IE provides information about which sub frames the sending eNB is configuring as almost blank subframes and which subset of almost blank subframes are recommended for configuring measurements towards the UE. Almost blank subframes are subframes with reduced power on some physical channels and/or reduced activity.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Invoke Indication
Content: This IE provides an indication about which type of information the sending eNB would like the receiving eNB to send back.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > MDT Configuration
Content: The IE defines the MDT configuration parameters.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > ABS Status
Content: The ABS Status IE is used to aid the eNB designating ABS to evaluate the need for modification of the ABS pattern.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Management Based MDT Allowed
Content: This information element is used by the eNB to allow selection of the UE for management based MDT as described in TS 32.422 .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > MultibandInfoList
Content: The MultibandInfoList IE contains the additional frequency band indicators that a cell belongs to listed in decreasing order of preference, see TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > M3 Configuration
Content: This IE defines the parameters for M3 measurement collection.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > M4 Configuration
Content: This IE defines the parameters for M4 measurement collection.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > M5 Configuration
Content: This IE defines the parameters for M5 measurement collection.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > MDT PLMN List
Content: The purpose of the MDT PLMN List IE is to provide the list of PLMNs allowed for MDT.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > EARFCN Extension
Content: The E-UTRA Absolute Radio Frequency Channel Number Extension defines the carrier frequency used in a cell for a given direction (UL or DL) in FDD or for both UL and DL directions in TDD.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > COUNT Value Extended
Content: This information element indicates the 15 bit long PDCP SN and the corresponding 17 bit long Hyper Frame Number.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Extended UL Interference Overload Info
Content: This IE provides report on interference overload for the set of subframes that are subject to UL-DL subframe reconfiguration. This IE applies to TDD only.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > RNL Header
Content: The RNL Header IE indicates the target eNB ID and source eNB ID.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Masked IMEISV
Content: This information element contains the IMEISV value with a mask, to identify a terminal model without identifying an individual Mobile Equipment.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Expected UE Behaviour
Content: This IE defines the behaviour of a UE with predictable activity and/or mobility behaviour, to assist the eNB/en-gNB in determining the optimum RRC connection time.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Expected UE Activity Behaviour
Content: Indicates information about the expected "UE activity behaviour" as defined in TS 23.401 .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > SeNB Security Key
Content: The SeNB Security Key IE is used to apply security in the SeNB as defined in TS 33.401 .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > SCG Change Indication
Content: The SCG Change Indication IE is either used to request the SeNB to prepare the SCG Change in the SeNB or to request the MeNB to initiate the SCG Change towards the UE (see TS 36.300 ).
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > CoMP Information
Content: This IE provides the list of CoMP hypothesis sets, where each CoMP hypothesis set is the collection of CoMP hypothesis(es) of one or multiple cells and each CoMP hypothesis set is associated with a benefit metric.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > CoMP Hypothesis Set
Content: This IE provides a set of CoMP hypotheses. A CoMP hypothesis is hypothetical PRB-specific resource allocation information for a cell.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > RSRP Measurement Report List
Content: This IE provides RSRP measurement reports of UEs served by the sending eNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Dynamic DL transmission information
Content: This IE contains assistance information for DL interference mitigation.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > ProSe Authorized
Content: This IE provides information on the authorization status of the UE for ProSe service(s).
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > CSI Report
Content: This IE provides CSI reports of UEs served by the cell for which the information is provided.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Wideband CQI
Content: This IE indicates the Wideband CQI as defined in TS 36.213 .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Subband CQI
Content: This IE indicates the Subband CQI as defined in TS 36.213 .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > COUNT Value for PDCP SN Length 18
Content: This information element indicates the 18 bit long PDCP SN and the corresponding 14 bit long Hyper Frame Number.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > LHN ID
Content: The LHN ID IE is used to indicate the LHN ID of the eNB, as defined in TS 23.003 .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Correlation ID
Content: This information element is the GTP Tunnel Endpoint Identifier or GRE key to be used for the user plane transport between eNB and the L-GW described in TS 23.401 .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > UE Context Kept Indicator
Content: This IE indicates that the UE Context at the SeNB is kept in case of inter-MeNB handover without SeNB/SgNB Change procedure, as specified in TS 36.300 or TS37.340 .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > eNB UE X2AP ID Extension
Content: This information element combined with the eNB UE X2AP ID uniquely identifies an UE over the X2 interface within an eNB. If the setup of an UE associcated signalling connection was initiated including the eNB UE X2AP ID Extension, the eNB UE X2AP ID Extension shall be used by both peers for the life-time of the respective UE-associated signalling connection. The usage of this IE is defined in TS 36.401 . NOTE: If X2-C signalling transport is shared among multiple interface instances, the value of the eNB UE X2AP ID, combined with the eNB UE X2AP ID Extension, is allocated so that it can be associated with an X2-C interface instance.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > M6 Configuration
Content: This IE defines the parameters for M6 measurement collection.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > M7 Configuration
Content: This IE defines the parameters for M7 measurement collection.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Tunnel Information
Content: The Tunnel Information IE indicates the transport layer address and UDP port number.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > X2 Benefit Value
Content: The X2 Benefit Value IE indicates the quantified benefit of the signalling connection.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Resume ID
Content: The Resume ID IE is used to address a suspended UE Context within an eNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Bearer Type
Content: This IE is used to support Non-IP data as specified in TS 23.401 .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > V2X Services Authorized
Content: This IE provides information on the authorization status of the UE to use the sidelink for V2X services.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Offset of NB-IoT Channel Number to EARFCN
Content: This IE is used to indicate the offset of the NB-IoT Channel Number to the EARFCN (TS 36.104 ).
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > WT UE XwAP ID
Content: The WT UE XwAP ID is allocated by the WT and uniquely identifies a UE over the Xw interface.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > UE Sidelink Aggregate Maximum Bit Rate
Content: This IE indicates the aggregate maximum bit rate for all radio bearers per UE in the sidelink for V2X services.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > NR Neighbour Information
Content: This IE contains cell configuration information of NR cells that a neighbour node may need for the X2 AP interface.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Extended Bit Rate
Content: This IE indicates the number of bits delivered by E-UTRAN in UL or to E-UTRAN in DL within a period of time, divided by the duration of the period. It is used, for example, to indicate the maximum or guaranteed bit rate for a GBR bearer, or an aggregated maximum bit rate.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > en-gNB UE X2AP ID
Content: This information element uniquely identifies an UE over the X2 interface within an en-gNB. The usage of this IE is defined in TS 36.401 . NOTE: If X2-C signalling transport is shared among multiple interface instances, the value of the en-gNB UE X2AP ID is allocated so that it can be associated with an X2-C interface instance.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > SgNB Security Key
Content: The SgNB Security Key IE is used to apply security in the en-gNB as defined in TS 33.401 .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Target SgNB ID Information
Content: This IE contains the target SgNB ID used by MeNB to find the target en-gNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > SCG Configuration Query
Content: The SCG Configuration Query IE is used to request the en-gNB to provide current SCG configuration.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Delivery Status
Content: This IE defines the Delivery Status IE of RRC Transfer message.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > NR Frequency Info
Content: The NR Frequency Info defines the carrier frequency and bands used in a cell for a given direction (UL or DL) in FDD or for both UL and DL directions in TDD.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > NR UE Security Capabilities
Content: This IE defines the supported algorithms for encryption and integrity protection in NR as defined in TS 33.401 . The eNBs store and send the complete bitmaps without modification or truncation as specified in TS 36.300 .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > EN-DC Resource Configuration
Content: This IE contains the EN-DC resource configuration for an E-RAB, indicating the presence of PDCP at the en-gNB and Lower Layers at MCG and SCG.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > PDCP Change Indication
Content: The PDCP Change Indication IE is used to require the MeNB to either initiate the security key update or to perform PDCP data recovery towards the UE (see TS 37.340 ).
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Served NR Cell Information
Content: This IE contains cell configuration information of an NR cell that a neighbour eNB may need for the X2 AP interface.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > NR CGI
Content: The NR Cell Global Identifier (NR CGI) is used to globally identify an NR cell (see TS 38.401 ).
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Global en-gNB ID
Content: This IE is used to globally identify an en-gNB (see TS 37.340 ).
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > NR Transmission Bandwidth
Content: The NR Transmission Bandwidth IE is used to indicate the UL or DL transmission bandwidth.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Cell Assistance Information
Content: The Cell Assistance Information IE is used by the eNB to request information about NR cells.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > MeNB Resource Coordination Information
Content: The MeNB Resource Coordination Information IE is LTE resource allocation at MeNB and used at the en-gNB to coordinate resource or sidelink resource utilisation between the MeNB and the en-gNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > SgNB Resource Coordination Information
Content: The SgNB Resource Coordination Information IE indicates resources within the bandwidth of the PCell which are not available for use by the MeNB and is used at the MeNB to coordinate resource utilisation between the en-gNB and the MeNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > UL Configuration
Content: This IE indicates how the UL PDCP is configured for the assisting node.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > RLC Mode
Content: The RLC Mode IE indicates the RLC Mode used for an E-RAB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Secondary RAT Usage Report List
Content: This IE provides information on the NR resources used with EN-DC as specified in TS 37.340 .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > UE Application layer measurement configuration
Content: The IE defines configuration information for the QoE Measurement Collection (QMC) function.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > DRB ID
Content: This information element uniquely identifies a DRB over the X2 interface within an en-gNB. The usage of this IE is defined in TS 36.331 .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > SUL Information
Content: This IE provides information about the SUL carrier.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Protected E-UTRA Resource Indication
Content: This IE indicates the resources allocated for E-UTRA DL and UL reference and control signals (hereby referred to as protected resources). This information is used in the process of E-UTRA NR Cell Resource Coordination.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Data Traffic Resource Indication
Content: This IE indicates the intended data traffic resource allocation for E-UTRA - NR Cell Resource Coordination.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Data Traffic Resources
Content: The Data Traffic Resources IE indicates the intended data traffic resource allocation for E-UTRA - NR Cell Resource Coordination.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Reserved Subframe Pattern
Content: The Reserved Subframe Pattern IE indicates the pattern of subframes in which the Protected E-UTRA Resource Indication and Data Traffic Resource Indication do not hold.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Aerial UE subscription information
Content: This information element is used by the eNB to know if the UE is allowed to use aerial UE function, refer to TS 23.401 .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > User plane traffic activity report
Content: This IE is used to indicate user plane traffic activity.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > RLC Status
Content: This IE indicates about the RLC configuration change included in the container towards the UE.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > RRC config indication
Content: This IE is used to indicate the type of RRC configuration used at the en-gNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > PDCP SN Length
Content: The PDCP SN Length IE is used to indicate the PDCP SN length configuration of the bearer.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Bluetooth Measurement Configuration
Content: This IE defines the parameters for Bluetooth measurement collection.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > WLAN Measurement Configuration
Content: This IE defines the parameters for WLAN measurement collection.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Subscription Based UE Differentiation Information
Content: This IE is generated by the MME based on the UE subscription information, it provides the Subscription Based UE differentiation Information.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Duplication activation
Content: The Duplication Activation IE indicates whether UL PDCP Duplication is activated or not.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > LCID
Content: This IE uniquely identifies a LCID for the associated DRB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > MeNB Coordination Assistance Information
Content: The MeNB Coordination Assistance Information IE is provided by the MeNB and used by the SgNB to determine further coordination of resource utilisation between the en-gNB and the MeNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > SgNB Coordination Assistance Information
Content: The SgNB Coordination Assistance Information IE is provided by the SgNB and used by the MeNB to determine further coordination of resource utilisation between the en-gNB and the MeNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Desired Activity Notification Level
Content: This IE contains information on which level activity notification shall be performed.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Location Information at SgNB
Content: The Location Information at SgNB IE enables the SgNB to provide the MeNB with information that supports localisation of the UE.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Interface Instance Indication
Content: The Interface Instance Indication identifies the interface instance the X2AP message is destined for. NOTE: The Interface Instance Indication is allocated so that it can be associated with an X2-C interface instance. The Interface Instance Indication may identify more than one interface instance.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > NB-IoT UL DL Alignment Offset
Content: This IE is used to indicate the offset between the UL carrier frequency center with respect to DL carrier frequency center.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Lower Layer presence status change
Content: This IE is used to indicate that lower layer resources’ presence status shall be changed. If the presence status is set to "release lower layers" or "suspend lower layers", PDCP entities, X2-U bearer resources, S1-U bearer resources and UE context information shall be kept.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Cell and Capacity Assistance Information
Content: The Cell and Capacity Assistance Information IE is used by the eNB to request information about NR or E-UTRA cells and it includes information about cell list size capacity.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Maximum Cell List Size
Content: This IE indicates the maximum size the sending node can handle for a given list.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Message Oversize Notification
Content: This IE indicates the maximum number of cells that can be received in the List of Served Cells NR IE.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > TNL Transport Layer Address Info
Content: This IE is used for signalling IP addresses of IP-Sec endpoints used for establishment of IP-Sec tunnels.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > CP Transport Layer Information
Content: This element is used to provide the transport layer information associated with EN-DC X2 control plane transport.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > TNL Association Usage
Content: This IE indicates the usage of the TNL association.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > RAN UE NGAP ID
Content: This IE uniquely identifies the UE association over the NG interface within the NG-RAN node, as specified in TS 38.413 .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > EPC Handover Restriction List Container
Content: This IE contains the Handover Restriction List IE specified in TS 36.413 as received by the E-UTRAN from the EPC.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > DAPS Request Information
Content: The DAPS Indicator IE indicates that the source eNB requests a DAPS HO for the concered E-RAB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > DAPS Response Information
Content: The DAPS Response Indicator IE indicates the response to a requested DAPS Handover for the concerned E-RAB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Maximum Number of CHO Preparations
Content: This IE indicates the maximum number of concurrently prepared CHO candidate cells for a UE at a candidate target eNB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Ethernet Type
Content: This IE is used to indicate that Ethernet data is expected.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > NR V2X Services Authorized
Content: This IE provides information on the authorization status of the UE to use the NR sidelink for V2X services.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > NR UE Sidelink Aggregate Maximum Bit Rate
Content: This IE provides information on the Aggregate Maximum Bitrate of the UE’s sidelink communication for NR V2X services.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > PC5 QoS Parameters
Content: This IE provides information on the PC5 QoS parameters of the UE’s sidelink communication for NR PC5.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > TNL Capacity Indicator
Content: The TNL Capacity Indicator IE indicates the available capacity of the Transport Network experienced by the NR cell.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > NR Radio Resource Status
Content: The NR Radio Resource Status IE indicates the usage of the PRBs per cell for MIMO and per SSB area for all traffic in Downlink and Uplink and the usage of PDCCH CCEs for Downlink and Uplink scheduling.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > NR Composite Available Capacity Group
Content: The NR Composite Available Capacity Group IE indicates the overall available resource level per cell and per SSB area in the cell in Downlink and Uplink.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > NR Composite Available Capacity
Content: The NR Composite Available Capacity IE indicates the overall available resource level in the cell in either Downlink or Uplink.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > NR Cell Capacity Class Value
Content: The NR Cell Capacity Class Value IE indicates the value that classifies the cell capacity with regards to the other cells. The NR Cell Capacity Class Value IE only indicates resources that are configured for traffic purposes.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > NR Capacity Value
Content: The NR Capacity Value IE indicates the amount of resources per cell and per SSB area that are available relative to the total en-gNB resources. The capacity value should be measured and reported so that the minimum en-gNB resource usage of existing services is reserved according to implementation. The NR Capacity Value IE can be weighted according to the ratio of cell capacity class values, if available.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > SSB Index
Content: The SSB Index IE identify an SSB area of an NR cell.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > NR Carrier List
Content: This IE indicates the SCS-specific carriers per TDD, per DL, per UL or per SUL of an NR cell.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > SSB Positions In Burst
Content: Indicates the time domain positions of the transmitted SS-blocks in a half frame with SS/PBCH blocks as defined in TS 38.213 , clause 4.1.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > NPRACH Configuration
Content: This IE indicates the NPRACH Configuration.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > UE Radio Capability ID
Content: This IE contains the UE Capability ID as defined in TS 23.003 .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > QoS Mapping Information
Content: This IE indicates the DSCP and/or IPv6 Flow Label field(s) of IP packets sent in the corresponding GTP-U tunnel for IAB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > UE Radio Capability
Content: This IE contains UE Radio Capability information.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > SFN Offset
Content: This IE contains the time offset between an absolute time reference and the SFN0 start. The IE is calculated assuming that the SFN transmission started at the absolute time reference. The absolute time reference chosen is 1980-01-06 T00:00:19 International Atomic Time (TAI).
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Global RAN Node ID
Content: This IE is used to globally identify an NG-RAN node.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > SCG UE History Information
Content: This IE contains information about the PSCells served by the secondary node in an active state.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > SCG Activation Status
Content: The SCG Activation Status IE indicates the status of the SCG resources.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > SCG Activation Request
Content: The SCG Activation Request IE indicates whether the SCG resources are required to be activated or deactivated .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Served Cell Specific Info Request
Content: The Served Cell Specific Info Request IE is used by the NG-RAN node to request specific information about NR cells.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Security Indication
Content: This IE contains the user plane integrity protection indication which indicates the requirements on UP integrity protection for the corresponding E-RAB.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Security Result
Content: This IE indicates whether the security policy indicated as "preferred" in the Integrity Protection Indication IE is performed or not.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Information Element definitions > Sensor Measurement Configuration
Content: This IE defines the parameters for Sensor measurement collection.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message and Information Element Abstract Syntax (with ASN.1) > General
Content: X2AP ASN.1 definition conforms to ITU-T Rec. X.680 and ITU-T Rec. X.681 . Sub clause 9.3 presents the Abstract Syntax of the X2AP protocol with ASN.1. In case there is contradiction between the ASN.1 definition in this sub clause and the tabular format in sub clause 9.1 and 9.2, the ASN.1 shall take precedence, except for the definition of conditions for the presence of conditional elements, in which the tabular format shall take precedence. The ASN.1 definition specifies the structure and content of X2AP messages. X2AP messages can contain any IEs specified in the object set definitions for that message without the order or number of occurrence being restricted by ASN.1. However, for this version of the standard, a sending entity shall construct an X2AP message according to the PDU definitions module and with the following additional rules: IEs shall be ordered (in an IE container) in the order they appear in object set definitions. Object set definitions specify how many times IEs may appear. An IE shall appear exactly once if the presence field in an object has value "mandatory". An IE may appear at most once if the presence field in an object has value "optional" or "conditional". If in a tabular format there is multiplicity specified for an IE (i.e. an IE list) then in the corresponding ASN.1 definition the list definition is separated into two parts. The first part defines an IE container list in which the list elements reside. The second part defines list elements. The IE container list appears as an IE of its own. For this version of the standard an IE container list may contain only one kind of list elements. NOTE: In the above, "IE" means an IE in the object set with an explicit ID. If one IE needs to appear more than once in one object set, then the different occurrences have different IE IDs. If an X2AP message that is not constructed as defined above is received, this shall be considered as Abstract Syntax Error, and the message shall be handled as defined for Abstract Syntax Error in clause 10.
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message and Information Element Abstract Syntax (with ASN.1) > Usage of Private Message Mechanism for Non-standard Use
Content: The private message mechanism for non-standard use may be used: for special operator (and/or vendor) specific features considered not to be part of the basic functionality, i.e. the functionality required for a complete and high-quality specification in order to guarantee multivendor inter-operability. by vendors for research purposes, e.g. to implement and evaluate new algorithms/features before such features are proposed for standardisation. The private message mechanism shall not be used for basic functionality. Such functionality shall be standardised.

-- -- ************************************************************** X2AP-PROTOCOL-IES-PAIR ::= CLASS \ \&id ProtocolIE-ID UNIQUE, \&firstCriticality Criticality, \&FirstValue, \&secondCriticality Criticality, \&SecondValue, \&presence Presence \ WITH SYNTAX \ ID \&id FIRST CRITICALITY \&firstCriticality FIRST TYPE \&FirstValue SECOND CRITICALITY \&secondCriticality SECOND TYPE \&SecondValue PRESENCE \&presence \ -- ************************************************************** -- -- Class Definition for Protocol Extensions -- -- Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Message transfer syntax
Content: X2AP shall use the ASN.1 Basic Packed Encoding Rules (BASIC-PER) Aligned Variant as transfer syntax, as specified in ITU-T Rec. X.691 .
Title Path: Elements for X2AP Communication > Timers
Content: T - Specifies the maximum time for the Handover Preparation procedure in the source eNB. TX2 - Specifies the maximum time for the protection of the overall handover procedure in the source eNB. T - Specifies the maximum time for the SeNB Addition Preparation, MeNB initiated SeNB Modification Preparation, SgNB Addition Preparation, or MeNB initiated SgNB Modification Preparation procedure in the MeNB. T - Specifies the maximum time in the SeNB for either the SeNB initiated SeNB Modification procedure or the protection of the E-UTRAN actions necessary to configure UE resources at SeNB Addition or MeNB initiated SeNB Modification. Or specifies the maximum time in the SgNB for either the SgNB initiated SgNB Modification procedure or the protection of the E-UTRAN actions necessary to configure UE resources at SgNB Addition or MeNB initiated SgNB Modification.
Title Path: Handling of unknown, unforeseen and erroneous protocol data
Content: Section 10 of TS 36.413 is applicable for the purposes of the present document.
